# Graph Report - upmind-monorepo  (2026-09-04)

## Corpus Check
- 8767 files · ~15,957,490 words
- Verdict: corpus is large enough that graph structure adds value.

## Summary
- 49181 nodes · 78518 edges · 3244 communities (2846 shown, 398 thin omitted)
- Extraction: 99% EXTRACTED · 1% INFERRED · 0% AMBIGUOUS · INFERRED: 1044 edges (avg confidence: 0.54)
- Token cost: 0 input · 0 output

## Graph Freshness
- Built from commit: `d2699e4d`
- Run `git rev-parse HEAD` and compare to check if the graph is stale.
- Run `graphify update .` after code changes (no API cost).
